Vicesimo tertio die [?Junii?] 1642

9o Junii 1643

[f. 132r]

21 Marcii 1643

This Booke containeth three thowsand seaven hundred Fiftye seaven horses geldings and mares, with what Armes was listed with them, the value of all which (according to [th]e sev[er]all enteries before goeing) doth amount to Fiftye fower thousand seaven hundred seaventye fower pownds Eighteen shillings foure pence, whereof wee discharge our selves as followeth in [th]e dischardge of our whole Accompt John Smythe Tho[mas] Richardson Comissaries

The 26th of Aprill 1644 M[aste]r Smyth & M[aste]r Richardson deliv[er]d this accompt upon their oathes Tho[mas] Hodges Rob[er]t Ellis

25 July 1650 The above said Thomas Richardson made Oath to [th]e truth of this Acco[mp]t Before us Henry Robinson Will[iam] Maddison

4 Dec[ember] 1651 The above named John Smyth this day made Oath to [th]e truth of this Acco[mp]t Before us Ri[chard] Wilcox Jo[hn] Greensmyth
